# Commands · I · part 1

1,418 entries, i-audit to indexer-health. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **i-audit**: Perform a comprehensive design quality audit on frontend code. Evaluates visual quality, interaction design, and — critically — detects telltale patterns of AI-generated "slop" aesthetics. ([Hamza-Haadi/disease-risk-classifier-hamza](https://github.com/Hamza-Haadi/disease-risk-classifier-hamza/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/i-audit.md) · 2 copies)

([Hamza-Haadi/disease-risk-classifier-hamza](https://github.com/Hamza-Haadi/disease-risk-classifier-hamza/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/i-harden.md) · 2 copies)

- **I-intel-scan**: 1. **立即加载** `@flow-kit/config/constitution.md`（全局最高优先级） 2. **立即加载** `@flow-kit/config/default-user-config.md`，若存在 `.flow-kit/user-config.md` 则优先加载用户自定义配置 3. 后续所有逻辑必须遵守这两份配置 ([pwl1987/flow-kit](https://github.com/pwl1987/flow-kit/blob/HEAD/flow-kit/commands/I-intel-scan.md) · ★1)
